Call of Duty: Modern Warfare 2 (Xbox 360 MP prototype, Jul 13 2009, title 41560817) offsets dump
========================================================================
Source XEX : default_mp.xex   MD5 809f791aaa2a9c26507792e2bed9dfd7
Image      : decrypted/decompressed PE, MD5 4454a663de2d31abba6cc1b07ec65303
Build      : "IW4 MP (Alpha) build ... xenon", "latest Mon Jul 13 2009 04:35:10AM"
Image base : 0x82000000 (all addresses are virtual; file offset in the
             decrypted/decompressed image = VA - 0x82000000)
Code range : 0x820A0000 .. 0x8242156C   (.text; .pdata at 0x8207B000, 11432 entries)
Arch       : PowerPC big-endian (Xenon). Args r3..r10, floats f1..f13;
             float args take up GPR slots (e.g. Dvar_RegisterFloat flags in r7).
Method     : static pass over every .pdata function (plus the leaf code between them),
             tracking lis/addi/ori/li/mr/lfs constants into each bl; results spot-checked
             with capstone disassembly.
"(inferred)" = name or meaning taken from behaviour/shape, not from a string.
Not covered on purpose: signature checks, RSA, hashes, anti-tamper/anti-cheat,
copy protection, Xbox LIVE auth, fastfile authentication.

Counts: functions 101 | globals 19 | console commands 316 (Cmd_AddCommand 283, inlined 4, Cmd_AddServerCommand 29, autocomplete 3) | dvar registrations 1530 (1503 unique names) | menu script commands 57

0. CBUF PROFILE (values for the console-command sender)
------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Cbuf_AddText guest address : 0x8226F590   (pdata bounds 0x8226F590..0x8226F6B8)
   signature                  : void Cbuf_AddText(int localClientNum, const char* text)
   first 0x20 bytes (prologue, the first bl is at +0x20):
      7D8802A6 9181FFF8 FBC1FFE8 FBE1FFF0 9421FF90 7C7E1B78 3860001B 7C9F2378
      mflr r12 / stw r12,-8(r1) / std r30,-0x18(r1) / std r31,-0x10(r1) / stwu r1,-0x70(r1) /
      mr r30,r3 / li r3,0x1B / mr r31,r4   ; +0x20: bl 0x8230FD78 (Sys_EnterCriticalSection 0x1B)
   cmd_text address formed at : +0x7C  0x8226F60C  3D2082FC  lis  r9, 0x82FC
                                +0x84  0x8226F614  39099E9C  addi r8, r9, -0x6164
                                (low half is negative, so the lis high half is 0x82FC, not 0x82FB)
   cmd_text (cmd_textArray[0]): 0x82FB9E9C
   layout                     : {u32 data; s32 maxsize; s32 cursize} = 12 bytes, same as Bo1;
                                entry = 0x82FB9E9C + localClientNum*12, 4 local clients (0..3).
                                client 0: data ptr @0x82FB9E9C (-> 0x82F79E18), maxsize @0x82FB9EA0
                                (0x10000), cursize @0x82FB9EA4.
   behaviour                  : appends strlen(text)+1 bytes at data+cursize (keeps the NUL), then
                                cursize += strlen(text); overflow if cursize+len >= maxsize
                                (prints "Cbuf_AddText: overflow\n" and drops the text). Leading
                                "p0".."p3 " (or "P0"..) re-targets the local client. End each
                                command with "\n" or ";"; Cbuf_Execute 0x82270DE0 splits on ; and
                                newlines and runs the lines on the next frame.
   how verified               : (1) "Cbuf_AddText: overflow\n" (0x82036144) has exactly one xref,
                                0x8226F658, inside the .pdata function 0x8226F590..0x8226F6B8.
                                (2) The prologue bytes above were read from the image and
                                disassembled with capstone; the lis/addi pair resolves to 0x82FB9E9C,
                                then +lc*12 is added and data/maxsize/cursize are read at +0/+4/+8.
                                (3) The same base 0x82FB9E9C is built in Cbuf_Init 0x8226F4A8 (which
                                writes {0x82F79E18+i*0x10000, 0x10000, 0} for i=0..3 and is called
                                from Com_Init right before Cmd_Init), Cbuf_InsertText 0x8226F6B8 and
                                Cbuf_ExecuteInternal 0x82270C88. (4) Callers pass text ending in
                                "\n": Com_Init (0,"exec default_mp.cfg\n"), UI_RunMenuScript
                                "disconnect\n", menu "exec" handler 0x822DF338 (args + "\n").
   immediate alternatives     : Cmd_ExecuteSingleCommand 0x82270978 (lc, controller, text) and
                                Cbuf_ExecuteBuffer 0x82270B90 (lc, controller, text; splits on ;).
                                controllerIndex for lc 0 = CL_ControllerIndexFromClientNum(0) 0x821A5848.

1. KEY ENGINE FUNCTIONS
------------------------------------------------------------------------
0x82270088  Cmd_AddCommand
    sig: void (const char* name, void (*fn)(), cmd_function_s* storage)
    how: 283 call sites with r3=name ("viewpos","togglescores","openmenu","bind","quit"...); calls Cmd_FindCommand, on a hit prints "Cmd_AddCommand: %s already defined" via Com_Printf, else links storage into list head 0x82F66DD0 (name at +4, fn at +0x10).
0x8226F7B0  Cmd_AddServerCommand
    sig: void (const char* name, void (*fn)(), cmd_function_s* storage)
    how: Leaf (no pdata entry). Same body as Cmd_AddCommand with Cmd_FindCommand inlined, but walks/links the second list head 0x82F66DCC; duplicate message "Cmd_AddServerCommand: %s already defined" (0x82036178). 29 callers (map, devmap, kick, map_restart, fast_restart, status...).
0x82270028  Cmd_FindCommand
    sig: cmd_function_s* (const char* name)
    how: Leaf (no pdata). strcmp loop over list 0x82F66DD0, returns node or NULL. Called first by Cmd_AddCommand and 4x by Cmd_Init.
0x822700F8  Cmd_RemoveCommand (inferred)
    sig: void (const char* name)
    how: Leaf (no pdata). Same strcmp walk over 0x82F66DD0 but unlinks the match (prev->next = node->next). 149 callers, usually paired with Cmd_AddCommand names (viewpos, togglescores, weapnext...).
0x82270160  Cmd_SetAutoComplete
    sig: void (const char* cmd, const char* dir, const char* ext)
    how: Cmd_FindCommand then stores dir/ext at +8/+0xC; called for map/devmap ("maps/mp","d3dbsp") and cinematic ("video","wmv").
0x82271098  Cmd_Init
    sig: void ()
    how: Called from Com_Init right after Cbuf_Init. Registers cmdlist/exec/vstr/wait with Cmd_AddCommand inlined (Cmd_FindCommand + manual insert into 0x82F66DD0, static nodes 0x82FBC734/0x82FBC720/0x82FBC70C/0x82FBC6F8).
0x822704B8  Cmd_List_f ("cmdlist" handler)
    sig: void ()
    how: Walks 0x82F66DD0, prints "%s\n" per command and "%i commands\n".
0x82270F48  Cmd_Exec_f ("exec" handler)
    sig: void ()
    how: "exec <filename> : execute a script file"; appends .cfg, tries Cmd_ExecFromDisk then Cmd_ExecFromFastFile, else Com_PrintError "couldn't exec %s".
0x82270E30  Cmd_ExecFromDisk (inferred)
    sig: bool (int localClientNum, int controllerIndex, const char* path)
    how: "execing %s from disk"; reads the file and runs it through Cbuf_ExecuteBuffer 0x82270B90.
0x82270EA8  Cmd_ExecFromFastFile (inferred)

0x8226F868  Cmd_Vstr_f ("vstr" handler)
    sig: void ()
    how: "vstr <variablename>"; Dvar_FindVar, then Cbuf_InsertText(va("%s\n", value)).
0x8226F438  Cmd_Wait_f ("wait" handler) (inferred)
    sig: void ()
    how: Sets cmd_wait 0x82F66DD8 to atoi(argv[1]) or 1.
0x8226FA40  Cmd_ArgvBuffer (inferred)
    sig: void (int arg, char* buffer, int bufferLength)
    how: Leaf. Copies cmd_args.argv[nesting][arg] (0x82F66D48 +0x64) into buffer; used by openmenu/closemenu handlers with r3=1, size 0x40. Cmd_Argv itself is inlined everywhere in this build.

    sig: void (const char* text, int maxTokens, CmdArgs* args, CmdArgsPrivate* priv)
    how: Called by Cmd_ExecuteSingleCommand with (text, 0x200-used, 0x82F66D48, 0x82FB9ED0) and by the server path with (0x82FB9E18, 0x82F66DE8).
0x8226F3F8  SV_Cmd_Argv (inferred)
    sig: const char* (int argIndex)
    how: Leaf. Same shape as Cmd_Argv but over sv_cmd_args 0x82FB9E18; returns "" when out of range.
0x8226F4A8  Cbuf_Init (inferred)
    sig: void ()
    how: Called first thing after the build banner in Com_Init. Under critical section 0x1B sets cmd_textArray[0..3] (0x82FB9E9C, 12 bytes each) = {0x82F79E18 + i*0x10000, 0x10000, 0} and the server-text buffer 0x82F66DDC = {0x82F69E18, 0x10000, 0}.
0x8226F590  Cbuf_AddText
    sig: void (int localClientNum, const char* text)
    how: Only xref of "Cbuf_AddText: overflow\n". Critical section 0x1B; optional "p0".."p3 " / "P0".. prefix overrides localClientNum; appends strlen+1 bytes at data+cursize of cmd_textArray[lc] (0x82FB9E9C + lc*12), cursize += strlen. 77 callers (menu "exec" handler, "exec default_mp.cfg\n", "disconnect\n").
0x8226F6B8  Cbuf_InsertText
    sig: void (int localClientNum, const char* text)
    how: Only xref of "Cbuf_InsertText overflowed\n"; same buffer array, memmove then copy at the front. Used by vstr.
0x82270DE0  Cbuf_Execute (inferred)
    sig: void (int localClientNum, int controllerIndex)
    how: Sets executing flag byte 0x82F69E10[lc], runs Cbuf_ExecuteInternal, clears it, then runs the server-text buffer (0x82270680). Com_Init calls it with (0, CL_ControllerIndexFromClientNum(0)) after Cbuf_AddText(0,"exec default_mp.cfg\n"); 14 callers.
0x82270C88  Cbuf_ExecuteInternal (inferred)
    sig: void (int localClientNum, int controllerIndex)
    how: Pops lines from cmd_textArray[lc] (lis 0x82FC / addi -0x6164), splits on ; (quote aware), \n, \r, max 0xFFF per line, honours wait counter 0x82F66DD8, runs each line with Cmd_ExecuteSingleCommand.
0x82270978  Cmd_ExecuteSingleCommand (inferred)
    sig: void (int localClientNum, int controllerIndex, const char* text)
    how: Tokenizes into cmd_args 0x82F66D48, walks 0x82F66DD0 (moves the hit to the front); handler == Cbuf_AddServerText_f stub -> SV_WaitServer + Cmd_ExecuteServerCommand; else bctrl. Unknown words: Dvar_Command, then server game command, then CL_ForwardCommandToServer. Executes immediately (UI_SetActiveMenu uses it for "xstopparty\n", UI_RunMenuScript for "quit").
0x82270B90  Cbuf_ExecuteBuffer (split on ;) (inferred)
    sig: void (int localClientNum, int controllerIndex, const char* text)
    how: Copies text, splits on ; / newline and runs each piece now via Cmd_ExecuteSingleCommand. Used by menu "execnow", exec-from-disk/fastfile and "set systemlink 0; set splitscreen 0; ..." in UI_SetActiveMenu. 17 callers.
0x82270578  Cmd_ExecuteServerCommand (inferred)
    sig: void (const char* text)
    how: Tokenizes into sv_cmd_args 0x82FB9E18 / 0x82F66DE8, walks sv_cmd_functions 0x82F66DCC (move to front) and calls the handler.
0x8226F7A8  Cbuf_AddServerText_f (stub) (inferred)

5. DVARS (every Dvar_Register* call with a constant name)
------------------------------------------------------------------------
   "store" = address the returned dvar_t* is written to (read it, then dvar->current at +0xC).
